# Commands and argument types

Every literal and argument node from `reports/commands.json` has a row, including executable branches and redirects. Paths are kept intact for testing. The report uses ALL command selection; publish and unpublish are labeled integrated-server only. Debug/IDE-only commands are outside default dedicated-server command selection.

The dedicated dispatcher registers 92 native roots. Commands with pending runtime execution are `/advancement`, `/attribute`, `/execute`, `/bossbar`, `/clear`, `/clone`, `/compute`, `/damage`, `/data`, `/datapack`, `/debug`, `/dialog`, `/effect`, `/enchant`, `/experience`, `/xp`, `/fillbiome`, `/function`, `/item`, `/kill`, `/locate`, `/loot`, `/swing`, `/particle`, `/place`, `/playsound`, `/posteffect`, `/random`, `/recipe`, `/fetchprofile`, `/ride`, `/rotate`, `/schedule`, `/scoreboard`, `/version`, `/spectate`, `/spreadplayers`, `/stopsound`, `/stopwatch`, `/tag`, `/team`, `/tellraw`, `/test`, `/title`, `/trigger`, `/waypoint`, `/worldborder`, `/jfr`, `/perf`, `/transfer`. Their exact literal/argument paths remain listed below, with native requirements and redirects in the client tree. `/tick` uses the canonical tick manager and drains its ordered events before feedback. `/teammsg` delivers to live team members through the canonical shared scoreboard; actual reload and block-entity placement remain explicitly partial. `/publish` and `/unpublish` are absent on dedicated servers.

| ID | Feature | Vanilla source | Test | Vanilla | Ferraloom |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| commands.brigadier.parse-errors | Parse literal and argument nodes, detect incomplete commands and return cursor-aware syntax errors | `CommandDispatcher; Commands#performCommand` | | - | todo |
| commands.brigadier.redirect-fork | Execute redirects and forks with source transformations and command-fork limits | `ExecutionContext; ExecuteCommand` | | - | todo |
| commands.brigadier.permissions | Filter available command nodes by source permission checks | `Commands#sendCommands; PermissionCheck` | | - | todo |
| commands.brigadier.suggestions | Return context-aware argument and command suggestions with input cursor ranges | `ServerGamePacketListenerImpl#handleCustomCommandSuggestions; CommandDispatcher` | | - | todo |
| commands.selectors.filters | Apply selector type, name, tag, team, scores, predicate, NBT, distance, box and rotation filters | `EntitySelectorParser; EntitySelectorOptions` | | - | todo |
| commands.selectors.order-limit | Apply nearest, furthest, random and arbitrary selector ordering and result limits | `EntitySelectorParser; EntitySelector` | | - | todo |
| commands.coordinates.relative-local | Resolve absolute, relative and local coordinate triples with source rotation and centering | `WorldCoordinates; LocalCoordinates` | `commands::arguments::tests::argument_parser_vectors_match_java_errors_cursors_and_values` | pass | pass |
| commands.result.store | Store success and result into scores, bossbars, NBT or storage with scale and numeric type | `ExecuteCommand` | | - | todo |
| commands.context.anchor-dimension | Apply execution position, rotation, facing, anchor, entity and dimension source changes | `ExecuteCommand; CommandSourceStack` | `commands::source::tests::entity_source_and_dimension_changes_preserve_vanilla_fields` | pass | pass |
| commands.execution.return | Propagate command result and return behavior through functions and condition branches | `ReturnCommand; ExecutionContext` | `commands::execution::tests::return_callbacks_run_inline_and_discard_pending_siblings` | pass | todo |
| commands.execution.feedback | Route command feedback, failures and operator broadcasts according to source and rules | `CommandSourceStack` | `commands::feedback::tests::routing_components_and_supplier_evaluation_match_java`; `ferraloom-parity::commands::parity_command_game_rules` | pass | pass |
